<script>
  (function initSelector() {
    let currentScale = 1;
    const map = document.getElementById('floorMapContainer');
    const btnZoomIn = document.getElementById('btnZoomIn');
    const btnZoomOut = document.getElementById('btnZoomOut');
    const btnResetView = document.getElementById('btnResetView');

    if (btnZoomIn && btnZoomOut && btnResetView && map) {
      btnZoomIn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (currentScale < 1.4) {
          currentScale += 0.15;
          map.style.transform = `scale(${currentScale})`;
        }
      });
      btnZoomOut.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (currentScale > 0.8) {
          currentScale -= 0.15;
          map.style.transform = `scale(${currentScale})`;
        }
      });
      btnResetView.addEventListener('click', () => {
        currentScale = 1;
        map.style.transform = 'scale(1)';
      });
    }

    // Interactive Cell Multi-Select Logic
    const cellButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.cell-toggle');
    const selectedLabel = document.getElementById('selectedCellsLabel');
    const badgeQty = document.getElementById('badgeQty');
    const specSummary = document.getElementById('specSummaryLabel');
    const totalRate = document.getElementById('totalRateLabel');
    const deposit = document.getElementById('depositLabel');

    let selectedCells = [
      { id: 'B04-L2-01', price: 480000, vol: 1.8, weight: 350 },
      { id: 'B04-L2-02', price: 480000, vol: 1.8, weight: 350 }
    ];

    function updateSummary() {
      const count = selectedCells.length;
      if (count === 0) {
        selectedLabel.textContent = 'Chưa chọn ô lưu trữ';
        badgeQty.textContent = '0 CELLS';
        specSummary.textContent = 'Vui lòng chọn ít nhất 1 ô trên kệ';
        totalRate.textContent = '₫0';
        deposit.textContent = '₫0';
        return;
      }

      const ids = selectedCells.map(c => c.id).join(', ');
      selectedLabel.textContent = `${count} Ô được chọn: ${ids}`;
      badgeQty.textContent = `${count} ${count > 1 ? 'CELLS' : 'CELL'}`;

      const totalVol = selectedCells.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.vol, 0).toFixed(1);
      const totalWeight = selectedCells.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.weight, 0);
      specSummary.textContent = `Tổng dung tích: ${totalVol} m³ • Chịu tải: Max ${totalWeight} kg`;

      const totalPrice = selectedCells.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.price, 0);
      totalRate.textContent = '₫' + totalPrice.toLocaleString('vi-VN');
      deposit.textContent = '₫' + totalPrice.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' (1 Tháng)';
    }

    cellButtons.forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const cellId = btn.getAttribute('data-cell-id');
        const price = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-price') || '480000', 10);
        const vol = parseFloat(btn.getAttribute('data-vol') || '1.8');
        const weight = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-weight') || '350', 10);

        const existingIndex = selectedCells.findIndex(c => c.id === cellId);

        if (existingIndex > -1) {
          // Deselect
          selectedCells.splice(existingIndex, 1);
          btn.classList.remove('bg-tertiary-fixed', 'text-on-tertiary-fixed', 'active-cell');
          btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary');
          btn.innerHTML = `
            <span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">${cellId}</span>
            <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary font-medium mt-0.5">Trống</span>
            <span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant">₫${(price / 1000)}k/th</span>
          `;
        } else {
          // Select
          selectedCells.push({ id: cellId, price, vol, weight });
          btn.classList.add('bg-tertiary-fixed', 'text-on-tertiary-fixed', 'active-cell');
          btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary');
          btn.innerHTML = `
            <div class="flex items-center gap-0.5">
              <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[13px] text-on-tertiary-fixed">check_circle</span>
              <span class="font-label-md text-label-md font-bold">${cellId}</span>
            </div>
            <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-semibold mt-0.5">Đã chọn</span>
            <span class="font-label-sm text-[10px] text-on-tertiary-fixed-variant">₫${(price / 1000)}k/th</span>
          `;
        }
        updateSummary();
      });
    });
  })();
</script></main>

</body></html>

<script>
  function filterRentals(category, buttonEl) {
    // Highlight clicked filter
    const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn');
    buttons.forEach(btn => {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
      btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface-variant');
    });
    buttonEl.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface-variant');
    buttonEl.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');

    // Filter cards
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.rental-card');
    cards.forEach(card => {
      if (category === 'all' || card.getAttribute('data-category') === category) {
        card.style.display = 'flex';
      } else {
        card.style.display = 'none';
      }
    });
  }

  function toggleAccessModal(isOpen) {
    const modal = document.getElementById('smart-pass-modal');
    if (isOpen) {
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else {
      modal.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 bg-surface-container-lowest/95 backdrop-blur-xl pb-safe shadow-[0_-2px_12px_rgba(16,61,43,0.06)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-body-md-medium"><div class="flex items-center justify-around h-16 px-1 max-w-[430px] mx-auto"><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="home-dashboard" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">roofing</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Home</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="explore-facilities" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">domain</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Explore</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 transition-colors text-primary font-body-md-medium" data-path="my-rentals" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">key</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">My Rentals</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="inventory" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">inventory_2</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Inventory</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="profile-settings" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Profile</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
    function switchServiceMode(mode) {
      const selfBtn = document.getElementById('mode-self-btn');
      const managedBtn = document.getElementById('mode-managed-btn');
      const tooltip = document.getElementById('service-mode-tooltip');

      if (mode === 'self') {
        selfBtn.className = "flex-1 py-2 px-2.5 rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md text-center transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 bg-surface-container-lowest text-primary shadow-sm";
        managedBtn.className = "flex-1 py-2 px-2.5 rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md text-center transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface";
        tooltip.textContent = "Showing private keycard access units. Personal walkthrough and 24/7 self-service load-in.";
      } else {
        managedBtn.className = "flex-1 py-2 px-2.5 rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md text-center transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 bg-surface-container-lowest text-tertiary shadow-sm";
        selfBtn.className = "flex-1 py-2 px-2.5 rounded-lg font-label-md text-label-md text-center transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface";
        tooltip.textContent = "Showing managed warehouse spaces. Professional staff barcoding, handling, and courier dispatch.";
      }
    }

    function toggleFilter(btn) {
      const isSelected = btn.classList.contains('bg-primary');
      if (isSelected) {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
        btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        const icon = btn.querySelector('.material-symbols-outlined');
        if (icon) icon.classList.add('text-primary');
      } else {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        btn.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
        const icon = btn.querySelector('.material-symbols-outlined');
        if (icon) icon.classList.remove('text-primary');
      }
    }

    // District pills click handling
    document.querySelectorAll('.district-chip').forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
        document.querySelectorAll('.district-chip').forEach(c => {
          c.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
          c.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        });
        chip.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        chip.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
      });
    });

    // Clear search micro interaction
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('facility-search-input');
    const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clear-search-btn');

    searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      if (e.target.value.length > 0) {
        clearBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
        clearBtn.classList.add('flex');
      } else {
        clearBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        clearBtn.classList.remove('flex');
      }
    });

    clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      searchInput.value = '';
      clearBtn.classList.add('hidden');
      clearBtn.classList.remove('flex');
      searchInput.focus();
    });
  </script>

<script>
  (function initInventoryInteractions() {
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('skuSearchInput');
    const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearSearchBtn');
    const chips = document.querySelectorAll('.category-chip');
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.inventory-card');
    const noResults = document.getElementById('noResults');

    let currentCategory = 'All SKUs (6)';
    let searchQuery = '';

    function filterItems() {
      let visibleCount = 0;
      cards.forEach(card => {
        const category = card.getAttribute('data-category');
        const name = (card.getAttribute('data-name') || '').toLowerCase();
        const sku = (card.getAttribute('data-sku') || '').toLowerCase();

        const matchCategory = currentCategory.startsWith('All') || 
                              category.toLowerCase().includes(currentCategory.toLowerCase()) ||
                              (currentCategory === 'Beverages & Coffee' && category.includes('Beverages')) ||
                              (currentCategory === 'Packaging' && category.includes('Packaging'));

        const matchSearch = !searchQuery || name.includes(searchQuery) || sku.includes(searchQuery);

        if (matchCategory && matchSearch) {
          card.style.display = 'flex';
          visibleCount++;
        } else {
          card.style.display = 'none';
        }
      });

      if (noResults) {
        noResults.style.display = visibleCount === 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
      }
    }

    // Category click listeners
    chips.forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
        chips.forEach(c => {
          c.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary', 'active');
          c.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        });
        chip.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary', 'active');
        chip.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface-variant');
        currentCategory = chip.textContent.trim();
        filterItems();
      });
    });

    // Search query listener
    if (searchInput) {
      searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        searchQuery = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (clearBtn) {
          clearBtn.style.display = searchQuery.length > 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
        }
        filterItems();
      });
    }

    if (clearBtn && searchInput) {
      clearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        searchInput.value = '';
        searchQuery = '';
        clearBtn.style.display = 'none';
        searchInput.focus();
        filterItems();
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 bg-surface-container-lowest/95 backdrop-blur-xl pb-safe shadow-[0_-2px_12px_rgba(16,61,43,0.06)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-body-md-medium"><div class="flex items-center justify-around h-16 px-1 max-w-[430px] mx-auto"><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="home-dashboard" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">roofing</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Home</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="explore-facilities" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">domain</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Explore</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="my-rentals" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">key</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">My Rentals</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 transition-colors text-primary font-body-md-medium" data-path="inventory" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">inventory_2</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Inventory</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="profile-settings" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Profile</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple micro-interaction for Smart Pass
  const unlockBtn = document.getElementById('tapUnlockBtn');
  const toast = document.getElementById('unlockToast');
  const pinDisplay = document.getElementById('pinDisplay');
  let isTapped = false;

  if (unlockBtn && toast) {
    unlockBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      // Toggle visual pin state
      pinDisplay.textContent = '0829 - OPEN';
      pinDisplay.classList.add('text-primary-fixed');
      
      // Trigger toast
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none', 'translate-y-3');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');

      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none', 'translate-y-3');
        toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
        pinDisplay.textContent = '•• 8294 ••';
      }, 3000);
    });
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 bg-surface-container-lowest/95 backdrop-blur-xl pb-safe shadow-[0_-2px_12px_rgba(16,61,43,0.06)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-body-md-medium"><div class="flex items-center justify-around h-16 px-1 max-w-[430px] mx-auto"><a aria-current="page" class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 transition-colors text-primary font-body-md-medium" data-path="home-dashboard" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">roofing</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Home</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="explore-facilities" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">domain</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Explore</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="my-rentals" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">key</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">My Rentals</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="inventory" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">inventory_2</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Inventory</span></a><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="profile-settings" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">person</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm mt-0.5 tracking-tight">Profile</span></a></div></nav></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple Carousel Interaction
  (function() {
    let currentSlide = 0;
    const totalSlides = 3;
    const track = document.getElementById('galleryTrack');
    const counter = document.getElementById('slideCounter');
    const prevBtn = document.getElementById('prevSlide');
    const nextBtn = document.getElementById('nextSlide');
    const favoriteBtn = document.getElementById('favoriteBtn');
    const favIcon = document.getElementById('favIcon');
    const shareBtn = document.getElementById('shareBtn');
    const bookStorageBtn = document.getElementById('bookStorageBtn');

    function updateCarousel() {
      if (track) {
        track.style.transform = `translateX(-${currentSlide * 100}%)`;
      }
      if (counter) {
        counter.textContent = `${currentSlide + 1}/${totalSlides}`;
      }
    }

    if (prevBtn) {
      prevBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        currentSlide = (currentSlide > 0) ? currentSlide - 1 : totalSlides - 1;
        updateCarousel();
      });
    }

    if (nextBtn) {
      nextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        currentSlide = (currentSlide < totalSlides - 1) ? currentSlide + 1 : 0;
        updateCarousel();
      });
    }

    // Favorite button toggle
    let isFav = false;
    if (favoriteBtn && favIcon) {
      favoriteBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        isFav = !isFav;
        if (isFav) {
          favIcon.textContent = 'favorite';
          favIcon.classList.add('text-error');
          favIcon.style.fontVariationSettings = "'FILL' 1";
        } else {
          favIcon.textContent = 'favorite_border';
          favIcon.classList.remove('text-error');
          favIcon.style.fontVariationSettings = "'FILL' 0";
        }
      });
    }

    // Share button handler
    if (shareBtn) {
      shareBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (navigator.share) {
          navigator.share({
            title: 'Saigon South Vault Flagship - VaultSpace',
            text: 'Kho lưu trữ thông minh chuẩn an ninh tại Quận 7, TP.HCM',
            url: window.location.href,
          }).catch(() => {});
        } else {
          alert('Đã sao chép liên kết cơ sở kho Saigon South Vault vào khay nhớ tạm!');
        }
      });
    }

    // CTA interaction
    if (bookStorageBtn) {
      bookStorageBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        // Direct forward action trigger
        console.log('Navigating to Locker Selection Screen...');
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main></body></html>

<div class="px-margin py-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-space-md">
<div class="flex flex-col min-w-0">
<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface truncate" id="bar-unit-name">Ô U-204</span>

<script>
  function selectUnit(element, name, size, price, volume, payload, location) {
    // Reset other active cards
    document.querySelectorAll('.unit-card').forEach(card => {
      card.classList.remove('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'shadow-md');
      card.classList.add('shadow-sm');
      const btn = card.querySelector('.unit-btn');
      if (btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary', 'pointer-events-none');
        btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high', 'text-primary');
        btn.innerHTML = '<span>Chọn ô này</span><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">arrow_forward</span>';
      }
      const badge = card.querySelector('.absolute.top-0.right-0');
      if (badge) badge.remove();
    });

    // Mark current card active
    element.classList.add('ring-2', 'ring-primary', 'shadow-md');
    element.classList.remove('shadow-sm');
    
    // Add badge
    const badgeDiv = document.createElement('div');
    badgeDiv.className = 'absolute top-0 right-0 bg-primary text-on-primary font-label-sm text-label-sm px-3 py-1 rounded-bl-xl flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm';
    badgeDiv.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">check_circle</span><span>ĐANG CHỌN</span>';
    element.appendChild(badgeDiv);

    const btn = element.querySelector('.unit-btn');
    if (btn) {
      btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high', 'text-primary');
      btn.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary', 'pointer-events-none');
      btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">check</span><span>Đã giữ ô</span>';
    }

    // Update summary preview
    document.getElementById('summary-unit-name').textContent = 'Ô ' + name;
    document.getElementById('summary-volume').textContent = volume;
    document.getElementById('summary-payload').textContent = payload;
    document.getElementById('summary-note').textContent = 'Vị trí ' + location + '. Trang bị sẵn pallet nhựa, đèn cảm biến và ổ cắm nội bộ.';

    // Update bottom bar
    document.getElementById('bar-unit-name').textContent = 'Ô ' + name;
    document.getElementById('bar-unit-size').textContent = '(' + size + ')';
    document.getElementById('bar-unit-price').textContent = price.split('/')[0];
  }

  function handleContinueBooking() {
    const currentUnit = document.getElementById('bar-unit-name').textContent;
    const currentPrice = document.getElementById('bar-unit-price').textContent;
    alert('Đang tiến hành đặt chỗ cho ' + currentUnit + ' với giá ' + currentPrice + '/tháng. Chuyển sang màn hình kỳ hạn thuê.');
  }

  // Size filter interactive switching
  document.querySelectorAll('.size-chip').forEach(chip => {
    chip.addEventListener('click', function() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.size-chip').forEach(c => {
        c.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
        c.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface');
      });
      this.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface');
      this.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
    });
  });
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
    // Copy Reservation Reference interaction
    function copyRef() {
      const ref = "VS-8921-TD";
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(ref).then(() => {
        const feedbackEl = document.getElementById("copyFeedback");
        const iconEl = document.getElementById("copyIcon");
        if (feedbackEl && iconEl) {
          feedbackEl.textContent = "Đã chép!";
          iconEl.textContent = "check";
          setTimeout(() => {
            feedbackEl.textContent = "Sao chép";
            iconEl.textContent = "content_copy";
          }, 2000);
        }
      }).catch(() => {
        // Fallback prompt if clipboard API blocked
        const feedbackEl = document.getElementById("copyFeedback");
        if (feedbackEl) {
          feedbackEl.textContent = "VS-8921-TD";
        }
      });
    }

    // Dynamic Countdown Timer (24h simulation)
    (function initTimer() {
      let secondsRemaining = 23 * 3600 + 59 * 60 + 45;
      const timerDisplay = document.getElementById("countdownTimer");
      if (!timerDisplay) return;

      const interval = setInterval(() => {
        if (secondsRemaining <= 0) {
          clearInterval(interval);
          timerDisplay.textContent = "00:00:00 (Hết hạn)";
          return;
        }
        secondsRemaining--;
        const hours = Math.floor(secondsRemaining / 3600);
        const mins = Math.floor((secondsRemaining % 3600) / 60);
        const secs = secondsRemaining % 60;
        timerDisplay.textContent = 
          String(hours).padStart(2, '0') + ":" + 
          String(mins).padStart(2, '0') + ":" + 
          String(secs).padStart(2, '0');
      }, 1000);
    })();
  </script>

<script>
  // Simple micro-interaction for Duration Selector & Start Date toggles
  (function() {
    const plans = document.querySelectorAll('.plan-option');
    const depositDisplay = document.getElementById('sticky-deposit-amount');
    const btnToday = document.getElementById('btn-start-today');
    const btnCustom = document.getElementById('btn-start-custom');
    const dateDisplay = document.getElementById('active-date-display');
    const ctaBtn = document.getElementById('btn-submit-booking');

    // Date Toggle
    btnToday?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      btnToday.className = 'flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg bg-primary text-on-primary font-body-md-medium text-body-md-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all shadow-sm';
      btnCustom.className = 'flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-high text-on-surface font-body-md-medium text-body-md-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all';
      dateDisplay.textContent = 'Hôm nay (Nhận kho trong 2 giờ)';
    });

    btnCustom?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      btnCustom.className = 'flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg bg-primary text-on-primary font-body-md-medium text-body-md-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all shadow-sm';
      btnToday.className = 'flex-1 py-2.5 px-3 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-high text-on-surface font-body-md-medium text-body-md-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all';
      dateDisplay.textContent = 'Thứ Sáu, 01/11/2024 (Tùy chọn)';
    });

    // Plan Switching
    plans.forEach(plan => {
      plan.addEventListener('click', () => {
        plans.forEach(p => {
          p.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-low', 'shadow-sm');
          p.classList.add('bg-surface');
          const radio = p.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
          const outerCircle = p.querySelector('.w-5');
          if (radio && outerCircle) {
            radio.classList.add('hidden');
            outerCircle.classList.remove('bg-primary');
            outerCircle.classList.add('bg-surface-container-highest');
          }
        });

        plan.classList.remove('bg-surface');
        plan.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low', 'shadow-sm');
        const activeRadio = plan.querySelector('.radio-indicator');
        const activeOuterCircle = plan.querySelector('.w-5');
        if (activeRadio && activeOuterCircle) {
          activeRadio.classList.remove('hidden');
          activeOuterCircle.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-highest');
          activeOuterCircle.classList.add('bg-primary');
        }
      });
    });

    // CTA Click Feedback
    ctaBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const originalText = ctaBtn.innerHTML;
      ctaBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin text-[20px]">progress_activity</span><span>Đang xử lý...</span>';
      setTimeout(() => {
        ctaBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check</span><span>Đã giữ chỗ!</span>';
        setTimeout(() => {
          ctaBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
        }, 1500);
      }, 700);
    });
  })();
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  (function initSignInInteractions() {
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggle-password-btn');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
    const eyeIcon = document.getElementById('eye-icon');
    const biometricCard = document.getElementById('biometric-card');

    if (toggleBtn && passwordInput && eyeIcon) {
      toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        const isPassword = passwordInput.getAttribute('type') === 'password';
        passwordInput.setAttribute('type', isPassword ? 'text' : 'password');
        eyeIcon.textContent = isPassword ? 'visibility' : 'visibility_off';
      });
    }

    if (biometricCard) {
      biometricCard.addEventListener('click', function() {
        const originalText = biometricCard.querySelector('.font-body-lg-medium').textContent;
        const textElement = biometricCard.querySelector('.font-body-lg-medium');
        textElement.textContent = 'Đang quét sinh trắc học...';
        biometricCard.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high');
        
        setTimeout(() => {
          textElement.textContent = 'Xác thực thành công ✓';
          setTimeout(() => {
            textElement.textContent = originalText;
            biometricCard.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high');
          }, 1400);
        }, 800);
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main>

<script>
  function showToast(msg) {
    const toast = document.getElementById('statusToast');
    const toastMsg = document.getElementById('toastMsg');
    if (!toast || !toastMsg) return;
    toastMsg.textContent = msg;
    toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none', '-translate-y-2');
    toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none', '-translate-y-2');
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
    }, 2400);
  }

  function handleLogout() {
    if (confirm('Bạn có chắc chắn muốn đăng xuất khỏi VaultSpace trên thiết bị này không?')) {
      showToast('Đang đăng xuất...');
    }
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 inset-x-0 z-50 bg-surface-container-lowest/95 backdrop-blur-xl pb-safe shadow-[0_-2px_12px_rgba(16,61,43,0.06)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-body-md-medium"><div class="flex items-center justify-around h-16 px-1 max-w-[430px] mx-auto"><a class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[44px] py-1 text-on-surface-variant <!-- Toast Message -->
<div class="fixed top-16 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-label-md text-label-md flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg transition-opacity duration-300 opacity-0 pointer-events-none" id="toast-message">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary-fixed">check_circle</span>
<span id="toast-text">Thông báo</span>

<script>
  let isSigned = false;

  function showToast(text) {
    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-message');
    const toastText = document.getElementById('toast-text');
    toastText.innerText = text;
    toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
    toast.classList.add('opacity-100');
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-100');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
    }, 2800);
  }

  function toggleConsent(checked) {
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-sign-primary');
    if (isSigned) return;

    if (checked) {
      btn.disabled = false;
      btn.classList.remove('opacity-40', 'cursor-not-allowed');
      btn.classList.add('opacity-100', 'cursor-pointer');
    } else {
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.classList.add('opacity-40', 'cursor-not-allowed');
      btn.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'cursor-pointer');
    }
  }

  function triggerOtpSigning() {
    if (isSigned) {
      showToast('Hợp đồng đã hoàn tất chữ ký số của cả 2 bên!');
      return;
    }
    const modal = document.getElementById('otp-modal');
    modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    modal.classList.add('flex');
  }

  function closeOtpModal() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('otp-modal');
    modal.classList.add('hidden');
    modal.classList.remove('flex');
  }

  function confirmSignature() {
    closeOtpModal();
    isSigned = true;

    // Update Signer B Status
    document.getElementById('badge-status-b').innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]" style="font-variation-settings: \'FILL\' 1;">check_circle</span> Đã ký OTP Viettel';
    document.getElementById('badge-status-b').className = 'flex items-center gap-0.5 text-primary font-label-sm text-label-sm';

    document.getElementById('badge-b-indicator').innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[13px]" style="font-variation-settings: \'FILL\' 1;">check</span> ĐÃ KÝ SỐ';
    document.getElementById('badge-b-indicator').className = 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-container font-label-sm text-label-sm font-semibold flex items-center gap-1';

    document.getElementById('content-b-pending').classList.add('hidden');
    document.getElementById('content-b-pending').classList.remove('flex');
    document.getElementById('content-b-signed').classList.remove('hidden');
    document.getElementById('content-b-signed').classList.add('flex');

    // Update Bottom CTA
    const btn = document.getElementById('btn-sign-primary');
    const btnText = document.getElementById('btn-sign-text');
    btnText.innerText = 'Hợp đồng đã hoàn tất chữ ký số';
    btn.classList.remove('bg-primary');
    btn.classList.add('bg-secondary');
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" style="font-variation-settings: \'FILL\' 1;">verified</span> <span>Hợp đồng đã hoàn tất ký số</span>';

    showToast('Ký hợp đồng thành công! Đã cấp mã chứng thư lưu trữ.');
  }

  function handleDownloadPdf() {
    showToast('Đang tạo và tải bản sao PDF/A-1b có chứng thư số...');
  }

  function handleSendEmail() {
    showToast('Đã gửi liên kết văn bản tới linh.nguyen@saigoncraft.vn');
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  // Countdown simulation for 23h 45m
  (function initCountdown() {
    let totalSeconds = 23 * 3600 + 45 * 60;
    const timerEl = document.getElementById('countdown-timer');
    if (!timerEl) return;

    setInterval(() => {
      if (totalSeconds > 0) {
        totalSeconds--;
        const hours = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
        const mins = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
        const secs = totalSeconds % 60;
        timerEl.textContent = 
          String(hours).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + 
          String(mins).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + 
          String(secs).padStart(2, '0');
      }
    }, 1000);
  })();

  // Toast helper
  function showToast(message) {
    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-notif');
    const toastText = document.getElementById('toast-text');
    if (!toast || !toastText) return;
    toastText.textContent = message;
    toast.classList.remove('opacity-0');
    toast.classList.add('opacity-100', '-translate-y-1');
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', '-translate-y-1');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0');
    }, 2400);
  }

  // Refresh Status interaction (financial transparency: NO fake instant success)
  const refreshBtn = document.getElementById('btn-refresh-status');
  if (refreshBtn) {
    refreshBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const icon = document.getElementById('refresh-icon');
      if (icon) icon.classList.add('animate-spin');
      refreshBtn.disabled = true;

      setTimeout(() => {
        if (icon) icon.classList.remove('animate-spin');
        refreshBtn.disabled = false;
        showToast('Chưa nhận được ghi có từ Napas247. Đang tiếp tục lắng nghe...');
      }, 1200);
    });
  }

  function cancelReservation() {
    if (confirm('Bạn có chắc muốn hủy đơn đặt cọc cho kho U-204? Ô kho sẽ được mở lại cho khách hàng khác.')) {
      showToast('Đang xử lý hủy đơn đặt chỗ #VS-8921-TD...');
    }
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  (function() {
    // PIN Obfuscation Toggle
    const togglePinBtn = document.getElementById('togglePinBtn');
    const pinValue = document.getElementById('pinValue');
    const realPin = document.getElementById('realPin');
    const togglePinIcon = document.getElementById('togglePinIcon');
    const togglePinText = document.getElementById('togglePinText');

    let isPinRevealed = false;

    if (togglePinBtn && pinValue && realPin) {
      togglePinBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        isPinRevealed = !isPinRevealed;
        if (isPinRevealed) {
          pinValue.textContent = realPin.textContent;
          togglePinIcon.textContent = 'visibility_off';
          togglePinText.textContent = 'Ẩn';
        } else {
          pinValue.textContent = '•••• 8284';
          togglePinIcon.textContent = 'visibility';
          togglePinText.textContent = 'Hiện';
        }
      });
    }

    // Toast helper
    const toast = document.getElementById('toastNotification');
    const toastMsg = document.getElementById('toastMessage');
    const toastIcon = document.getElementById('toastIcon');
    let toastTimeout = null;

    function showToast(message, icon = 'check_circle') {
      if (!toast) return;
      if (toastTimeout) clearTimeout(toastTimeout);

      toastMsg.textContent = message;
      toastIcon.textContent = icon;
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-0');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-100');

      toastTimeout = setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.remove('opacity-100');
        toast.classList.add('opacity-0');
      }, 2400);
    }

    // Copy Contract Code
    const copyContractBtn = document.getElementById('copyContractBtn');
    if (copyContractBtn) {
      copyContractBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        navigator.clipboard?.writeText('#VS-HD-2024-884');
        showToast('Đã chép mã HĐ #VS-HD-2024-884');
      });
    }

    // Copy PIN
    const copyPinBtn = document.getElementById('copyPinBtn');
    if (copyPinBtn) {
      copyPinBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        navigator.clipboard?.writeText('208284');
        showToast('Đã chép mã PIN mở cửa: 208284', 'key');
      });
    }

    // NFC Trigger Simulation
    const nfcBtn = document.getElementById('nfcTriggerBtn');
    if (nfcBtn) {
      nfcBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        showToast('Đang quét cảm biến NFC tại cửa U-204...', 'contactless');
      });
    }

    // QR Trigger Simulation
    const qrBtn = document.getElementById('qrTriggerBtn');
    if (qrBtn) {
      qrBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        showToast('Mã QR một lần đã sẵn sàng tại lễ tân', 'qr_code_2');
      });
    }

    // Support Trigger
    const supportBtn = document.getElementById('supportBtn');
    if (supportBtn) {
      supportBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        showToast('Đang kết nối Quản lý Saigon South...', 'headset_mic');
      });
    }

    // Terminate Notice
    const terminateBtn = document.getElementById('terminateNoticeBtn');
    if (terminateBtn) {
      terminateBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        showToast('Đã ghi nhận yêu cầu chuẩn bị bàn giao kho', 'info');
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main></body></html>

<!-- Notification Toast -->
<div class="hidden fixed bottom-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface font-label-md text-label-md shadow-lg flex items-center gap-2" id="toastNotification">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] text-primary-fixed">check_circle</span>
<span id="toastMessage">Yêu cầu đã được chuyển tới thủ kho VaultSpace!</span>
</div>

<script>
  (function initInventoryDetail() {
    const btnRequestOutbound = document.getElementById('btnRequestOutbound');
    const outboundModal = document.getElementById('outboundModal');
    const btnCloseModal = document.getElementById('btnCloseModal');
    const btnMinus = document.getElementById('btnMinus');
    const btnPlus = document.getElementById('btnPlus');
    const inputQty = document.getElementById('inputQty');
    const btnConfirmSubmit = document.getElementById('btnConfirmSubmit');
    const toast = document.getElementById('toastNotification');
    const toastMsg = document.getElementById('toastMessage');

    const btnShare = document.getElementById('btnShare');
    const btnRequestInbound = document.getElementById('btnRequestInbound');
    const btnLedgerAudit = document.getElementById('btnLedgerAudit');
    const btnEditCatalog = document.getElementById('btnEditCatalog');

    function showToast(message) {
      if (!toast) return;
      toastMsg.textContent = message;
      toast.classList.remove('hidden');
      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.add('hidden');
      }, 3200);
    }

    if (btnRequestOutbound && outboundModal) {
      btnRequestOutbound.addEventListener('click', () => {
        outboundModal.classList.remove('hidden');
      });
    }

    if (btnCloseModal && outboundModal) {
      btnCloseModal.addEventListener('click', () => {
        outboundModal.classList.add('hidden');
      });
    }

    if (btnMinus && inputQty) {
      btnMinus.addEventListener('click', () => {
        let val = parseInt(inputQty.value, 10) || 1;
        if (val > 1) {
          inputQty.value = val - 1;
        }
      });
    }

    if (btnPlus && inputQty) {
      btnPlus.addEventListener('click', () => {
        let val = parseInt(inputQty.value, 10) || 1;
        if (val < 220) {
          inputQty.value = val + 1;
        }
      });
    }

    if (btnConfirmSubmit && outboundModal) {
      btnConfirmSubmit.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const qty = inputQty ? inputQty.value : 20;
        outboundModal.classList.add('hidden');
        showToast('Đã tạo lệnh xuất kho ' + qty + ' túi! Thủ kho đang xác nhận.');
      });
    }

    if (btnShare) {
      btnShare.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (navigator.share) {
          navigator.share({
            title: 'VaultSpace SKU-CF-0891',
            text: 'Highland Arabica Coffee Beans - Ô B04-L2-01 East Metro Hub',
            url: window.location.href,
          }).catch(() => {});
        } else {
          showToast('Đã sao chép mã SKU-CF-0891 vào bộ nhớ tạm.');
        }
      });
    }

    if (btnRequestInbound) {
      btnRequestInbound.addEventListener('click', () => {
        showToast('Đang mở biểu mẫu hẹn lịch nhập hàng mới cho thủ kho...');
      });
    }

    if (btnLedgerAudit) {
      btnLedgerAudit.addEventListener('click', () => {
        showToast('Đang tải biên bản kiểm đếm và chữ ký số đợt 20/10...');
      });
    }

    if (btnEditCatalog) {
      btnEditCatalog.addEventListener('click', () => {
        showToast('Mở giao diện sửa mô tả và ghi chú lưu kho SKU...');
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main></body></html>

<button class="w-full h-12 rounded-xl bg-primary text-on-primary font-headline-sm text-headline-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:bg-on-primary-fixed-variant transition-colors shadow-md" id="confirm-booking-btn" type="button">
<span>Xác nhận đặt chỗ (₫960.000)</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">arrow_forward</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
<script>
  (function() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('confirm-booking-btn');
    const checkbox = document.getElementById('policy-agree');
    
    if (checkbox && btn) {
      checkbox.addEventListener('change', () => {
        if (!checkbox.checked) {
          btn.classList.add('opacity-50', 'pointer-events-none');
        } else {
          btn.classList.remove('opacity-50', 'pointer-events-none');
        }
      });
    }

    if (btn) {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin text-[20px]">progress_activity</span><span>Đang xử lý đơn...</span>';
        setTimeout(() => {
          btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check</span><span>Đã xác nhận đặt chỗ!</span>';
          btn.classList.replace('bg-primary', 'bg-primary-container');
        }, 800);
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main></body></html>
